Diagnostic Report

Diagnostic report requests and examples
Changelog v20.22.0.x
Getting Started Authorization Allergy Intolerance Care Plan Care Team Condition Diagnostic Report Document Reference Encounter Goals Immunization Medication Observation Organization Patient Practitioner Procedure Provenance

Terminology Bindings

Terminology Detail
DiagnosticReport.category A code that classifies the clinical discipline, department or diagnostic service that created the report.
DocumentReference.code A categorization for the type of document referenced - helps for indexing and searching.

Parameters

Parameter Is Required Description
Id This, or patient. The logical resource ID associated with the resource.
patient This, or ID The patient who has the diagnostic report.
category No The category for the diagnostic report. Example: category=LP29684-5.
code No The code for the diagnostic report. Example: code=LP29684-5.
date No Date range into which the diagnostic report falls. Example: date=ge2020-01-01.
_revinclude No A request to include any Provenance resource in the bundle.

Diagnostic Report Requests

Diagnostic Report by Patient, Category, Date, and Code

Patient:
 GET: 'https://api.practicestudio.net/patientportal/fhir/DiagnosticReport?patient={Patient_ID}'

Patient, Category, and Date:
 GET: 'https://api.practicestudio.net/patientportal/fhir/DiagnosticReport?category=LP29684-5&date=2012-06-05&patient={Patient_ID}'

Patient and Category:
 GET: 'https://api.practicestudio.net/patientportal/fhir/DiagnosticReport?category=LP29684-5&patient={Patient_ID}'

Patient and Code:
 GET: 'https://api.practicestudio.net/patientportal/fhir/DiagnosticReport?code=LP29684-5&patient={Patient_ID}'

Diagnostic Report by Resource ID

GET: 'https://api.practicestudio.net/patientportal/fhir/DiagnosticReport/{Resource_ID}'
